/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_FIM);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_FIM AS
   SELECT 
            (COUNT(t1.CdDimContaCartao)) AS COUNT_of_CdDimContaCartao,
          
            (SUM(t1.LIMITE_ATUAL)) FORMAT=BEST7. AS SUM_of_LIMITE_ATUAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.LIMITE_NOVO)) AS SUM_of_LIMITE_NOVO,
          t1.CLUSTER_CORES_V2,
          t1.GRUPO_RISCO_lake2,
          t1.DESCRICAO
      FROM WORK.BASE_FIM t1
      GROUP BY t1.CLUSTER_CORES_V2,
               t1.GRUPO_RISCO_lake2,
               t1.DESCRICAO;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */

/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */

/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_VALIDA);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_VALIDA AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CdDimContaCartao)) AS COUNT_of_CdDimContaCartao,
          
            (SUM(t1.Em_Dia_SCR)) AS SUM_of_Em_Dia_SCR,
          
            (SUM(t1.Em_Atraso_SCR)) AS SUM_of_Em_Atraso_SCR,
          
            (SUM(t1.Em_Prejuizo_SCR)) AS SUM_of_Em_Prejuizo_SCR,
          
            (SUM(t1.saldo_a_vencer_scr)) AS SUM_of_saldo_a_vencer_scr,
          
            (SUM(t1.Dia_Ate30d_BV_SCR)) AS SUM_of_Dia_Ate30d_BV_SCR,
          t1.perfil_compra,
          
            (SUM(t1.parcelado_scr)) AS SUM_of_parcelado_scr,
          
            (SUM(t1.SALDO_ATE30D_2)) AS SUM_of_SALDO_ATE30D_2,
          
            (SUM(t1.Rotativo_30D)) AS SUM_of_Rotativo_30D,
          
            (SUM(t1.Dia_Ate30d)) AS SUM_of_Dia_Ate30d
      FROM WORK.VALIDA t1
      GROUP BY t1.SAFRA,
               t1.perfil_compra;
QUIT;

	                X1 = 'HEADER ';

	                X3 = &SEQUENCIAL.;

		KEEP FINAL;

	count(*) AS qte
	FROM base_envio;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUM_BAU_IMPLANTACAO_20251118 AS
SELECT
	   A.CPF,
	   A.cddimcontacartao,
	   b.DESCRICAO,
	   A.'Limite Atual'n as Limite_Atual,
	   B.Limite_Atual AS Limite_Anterior,
	   c.CLUSTER_CORES_V2,
	   c.GRUPO_RISCO_lake2,
	   C.VrSaldoRiscoContaCartao,
	   C.BANDEIRA

FROM IMPLANTACAO_RECEPTIVO_20251118 AS A
LEFT JOIN GALDINO.SUCESSO_TESTE_CARGA_NOV25 AS B
ON A.cddimcontacartao = B.cddimcontacartao
left join MANUTLIM._20251117_BASE_FIM_POL_AUMENTO as c
ON A.cddimcontacartao = c.cddimcontacartao
;
QUIT;


PROC FREQ DATA=AUM_BAU_IMPLANTACAO_20251118; TABLE DESCRICAO CLUSTER_CORES_V2 GRUPO_RISCO_lake2; RUN;


DATA MANUTLIM.AUM_BAU_IMPLANTACAO_20251118(COMPRESS=YES REUSE=YES);
SET AUM_BAU_IMPLANTACAO_20251118;

FORMAT DtProcesso DDMMYY10.;
DTPROCESSO = TODAY();

RUN;


PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CUBO_IMPLANTACAO AS SELECT
COUNT(CDDIMCONTACARTAO) AS COUNT_of_cdconta,
DESCRICAO,
SUM(Limite_Atual) AS SUM_of_Limite_Atual,
SUM(Limite_Anterior) AS SUM_of_Limite_Anterior,
CLUSTER_CORES_V2,
GRUPO_RISCO_lake2,
BANDEIRA,
SUM(VrSaldoRiscoContaCartao) AS SUM_of_VrSaldoRiscoContaCartao

FROM MANUTLIM.AUM_BAU_IMPLANTACAO_20251118
GROUP BY
	DESCRICAO,
	CLUSTER_CORES_V2,
	GRUPO_RISCO_lake2,
	BANDEIRA;
RUN;


PROC FREQ DATA=MANUTLIM.MANUTENCAO_TOTAL;
TABLE DESCRICAO*DTPROCESSO
	DESCRICAO*ANOMES_REF
	DESCRICAO*ANOMES
/ MISSING NOCOL NOCUM NOROW NOPERCENT;
WHERE ANOMES_REF >= 202507;
RUN;

PROC SQL;

            (COUNT(t1.cdconta)) AS COUNT_of_cdconta,

               t1.CLUSTER_CORES_V2,
               t1.GRUPO_RISCO_lake2,
               t1.aprovado_mes_ant;

DATA IMPLANTACAO;
SET BASE_2_ONDA_ACEITESV3;

LIMITE_FINAL = (LIMITE_NOVO);

NuCPFCNPJ = put(CPF,z11.);
WHERE JA_IMPLANTADO = 0;

RUN;


	data BASE_DXC;
	set IMPLANTACAO;
		cddimconta = put(cddimcontacartao, z11.);
		nucontacartao = substr (cddimconta,3)*1;
		produto1 = substr (cddimconta,1,2)*1;
	run;
	

	data base_envio;
	set BASE_DXC;
	sequencia=_N_;
	run;

	
	%LET SEQUENCIAL= '945.IN '; 

	
	DATA Header;
	LENGTH FINAL $1021.;
	                X1 = 'HEADER '; 
	                X2 = 'MASS1001MLCRDI000'; 
	                X3 = &SEQUENCIAL.; 
	                X4 = substr(put(year(today()),z4.),1,4)||substr(put(month(today()),z2.),1,2) || substr(put(day(today()),z2.),1,2) ;
	                X5 = put(compress(put(time (),time8.),":")*1, z6.);
	                X6 = '000000D5D789D00959A8B6000000000000000';
	                FINAL = X1||X2||X3||X4||X5||X6;
	                KEEP FINAL;
	RUN;

	
	DATA Arquivo;
		SET base_envio;
		LENGTH FINAL $1021.;
		X1 = 'BVFINANCEIRA    ';
		X2 = 'D5D789D00959A8B6';
		X3 = PUT(_N_,Z7.);
		X4 = PUT(_N_,Z9.);
		X5 = PUT(14,Z4.);
		X6 = PUT(1001,Z4.);
		X7 = PUT(1,z5.);
		X8 = PUT(produto1,Z2.);
		X9 = '                              ';
		X10 = PUT(1,Z2.);
		X11 = '       ';
		X12 = '                ';  
		X13 = PUT(986,Z4.);
		X14 = PUT(LIMITE_FINAL,Z14.);
		X14_2 = PUT(0,Z4.);
		X15 = PUT(0,Z18.);
		X16 = PUT(0,Z1.);
		X17 = '                  ';
		X18 = ' ';
		X19 = '                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                ';
		FINAL = X1||X2||X3||X4||X5||X6||X7||X8||X9||X10||NuCPFCNPJ||X11||X12||X13||X14||X14_2||X15||X16||X17||X18||X19;
		KEEP FINAL;
	RUN;

	
	PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE Trail AS
	SELECT
	count(*) AS qte
	FROM base_envio;
	QUIT;

	DATA trailler;
	    SET trail;
	    LENGTH FINAL $1021.;
	    X1 = 'TRAILER'; 
	    X2 = 'MASS1001MLCRDI000'; 
	    X3 = &SEQUENCIAL.; 
	    X4 = substr(put(year(today()),z4.),1,4)||substr(put(month(today()),z2.),1,2) || substr(put(day(today()),z2.),1,2) ;
	    X5 = put(compress(put(time (),time8.),":")*1, z6.);
	    X6 = '000000D5D789D00959A8B600';
	    X7 = put((qte+2),z7.);
	    X8 = '000000';
	    FINAL = X1||X2||X3||X4||X5||X6||X7||X8;
	    KEEP FINAL;

	RUN;

	
	DATA BASE_AUMENTO_HP_NOVO;
	SET Header Arquivo Trailler;
	LENGTH FINAL $1021.;
	run;

	PROC EXPORT DATA=BASE_AUMENTO_HP_NOVO
	            OUTFILE="/riscosbvf/pol_empcr/Massivo/MASS1001MLCRDI000945.IN" 
	            DBMS=dlm REPLACE;
	            DELIMITER='3B'x;
	            PUTNAMES=NO;
	RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CUBO_RESUMO_GERAL_IMPL AS SELECT
DESCRICAO,
CLUSTER_CORES_V2,
GRUPO_RISCO_lake2,
COUNT(*) AS QTD_CONTAS,
SUM(Limite_Anterior) AS LIMITE_ATUAL,
SUM(LIMITE_ATUAL) AS LIMITE_NOVO
FROM base_final
GROUP BY 1,2,3;
RUN;

   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_HISTORICO6_0000 AS
   SELECT 
            (COUNT(t1.cdconta)) AS COUNT_of_cdconta,
          
            (SUM(t1.Limite_Atual)) FORMAT=BEST9. AS SUM_of_Limite_Atual,
          
            (SUM(t1.Limite_Anterior)) FORMAT=BEST7. AS SUM_of_Limite_Anterior,
          t1.CLUSTER_CORES_V2,
          t1.GRUPO_RISCO_lake2,
          
            (SUM(t1.VrSaldoRiscoContaCartao)) FORMAT=17.2 AS SUM_of_VrSaldoRiscoContaCartao,
          t1.aprovado_mes_ant,
          t1.DESCRICAO
      FROM WORK.HISTORICO t1
      GROUP BY t1.CLUSTER_CORES_V2,
               t1.GRUPO_RISCO_lake2,
               t1.aprovado_mes_ant,
               t1.DESCRICAO;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE CUBO3 AS
   SELECT

          GRUPO_RISCO2,
          CAMADAS_RENTABILIZAR,
          FX_PERC_ENVIDIDAMENTO,
          FX_IU,

          CASCATA_RENT,
          CASCATA_NOVO,
		  BANDEIRA,
		  FL_OVERLIMIT,
		  FL_AUM,
		  FL_AUM_2,
		  flag_Controle_desafiante,
          pub_elegivel_aumento as pub_elegivel_aumento2,





        COUNT(CdDimContaCartao) AS QTD,
        SUM(VrSaldoRiscoContaCartao) AS SALDO_RISCO,
        SUM(VrLimiteContaCartaoGeral2) AS LIMITE_ATUAL,
        SUM(Em_Dia_SCR) AS SALDO_RISCO_CARTAO_SCR,


        SUM(LIMITE_POTENCIAL2) AS LIMITE_POTENCIAL2,
        SUM(CMP_rotativo) AS CMP_rotativo,
        SUM(IU) AS IU,
        SUM(DEMANDA) AS DEMANDA,
        SUM(LIMITE_REFERENCIA) AS LIMITE_REFERENCIA,
        SUM(INTERVALO) AS INTERVALO,
        SUM(PERC_INTERVALO) AS PERC_INTERVALO,
        SUM(DEMANDA_OVER) AS DEMANDA_OVER,
        SUM(PERC_TETO) AS PERC_TETO,
        SUM(TETO_LIMITE_PERC) AS TETO_LIMITE_PERC,
        SUM(VALOR_TETO) AS VALOR_TETO,
        SUM(TETO_LIMITE_VALOR) AS TETO_LIMITE_VALOR,
        SUM(TETO_FINAL) AS TETO_FINAL,
        SUM(PACE_AUMENTO) AS PACE_AUMENTO,
        SUM(VALOR_AUMENTO) AS VALOR_AUMENTO,
        SUM(LIMITE_PROPOSTA) AS LIMITE_PROPOSTA,
        SUM(LIMITE_FINAL) AS LIMITE_FINAL,
        SUM(LIMITE_FINAL_3) AS LIMITE_FINAL_2,
        SUM(PISO_LIMITE_PERC) AS PISO_LIMITE_PERC,
     	SUM(PISO_FINAL) AS PISO_FINAL,
        SUM(DELTA_EXP) AS DELTA_EXP,
       	SUM(VR_LIMITE_FINAL) AS VR_LIMITE_FINAL,
		SUM(TETO) AS TETO,
		SUM(PISO) AS PISO,
		MIN(LIMITE_FINAL_3) AS LIMITE_FINAL_MIN,
		MAX(LIMITE_FINAL_3) AS LIMITE_FINAL_MAX,
		MIN(DELTA_EXP) AS DELTA_EXP_MIN,
		MAX(CASE WHEN LIMITE_FINAL_3 <= 1000 THEN . ELSE DELTA_EXP END) AS DELTA_EXP_MAX,
		MAX(CASE WHEN LIMITE_FINAL_3 <= 1000 THEN . ELSE DELTA_EXP_PRE END) AS DELTA_EXP_PRE_MAX,
		MIN(DELTA_EXP_PRE) AS DELTA_EXP_PRE_MIN

      FROM MANUTLIM.BASE_LOG_RENT_20251117
      GROUP BY

          GRUPO_RISCO2,
          CAMADAS_RENTABILIZAR,
          FX_PERC_ENVIDIDAMENTO,
          FX_IU,

          CASCATA_RENT,
          CASCATA_NOVO,
		  BANDEIRA,
		  FL_OVERLIMIT,
		  FL_AUM,
		  FL_AUM_2,
		  flag_Controle_desafiante,
		  pub_elegivel_aumento
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE POLITICA_BAU  AS
SELECT
A.CdDimContaCartao,
a.CPF,
a.VrLimiteContaCartaoGeral,
a.VrLimiteContaCartaoGeral2 as LIMITE_ATUAL,
A.limite_rodada2 as LIMITE_NOVO,
'POLITICA_BAU' AS DESCRICAO

FROM MANUTLIM._20250519_BASE_FIM_POL_AUMENTO AS A
where CASCATA in ('99.ELEGIVEL')
	and flag_controle_bau NOT IN ('Controle')
;
QUIT;



















PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE POLITICA_RECEPTIVO AS
SELECT
A.CdDimContaCartao,
a.nucpf AS CPF,
a.VrLimiteContaCartaoGeral,
a.limite_atual as LIMITE_ATUAL,
A.limite_receptivo5 as LIMITE_NOVO,

'RECEPTIVO' AS DESCRICAO

FROM MANUTLIM._20250519_BASE_FIM_POL_RECEPT AS A
WHERE
motivo_recusa IN ('21_Elegível')
and flag_Controle_recep ne 'Controle'

;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE POLITICA_DESAFIANTE_RENTABILIZAR  AS
SELECT
A.CdDimContaCartao,
a.CPF,
a.VrLimiteContaCartaoGeral,
a.VrLimiteContaCartaoGeral2 as LIMITE_ATUAL,
A.limite_final_3 as LIMITE_NOVO,
'RENTAB_DESAF' AS DESCRICAO

FROM MANUTLIM.BASE_LOG_RENT_20250519 AS A
WHERE CASCATA_NOVO IN ('29.ELEGIVEIS')
	AND SAIDA_RISCO_NOVO NOT IN ('D: LARANJA', 'E: VERMELHO')
	AND flag_Controle_desafiante = 'Desafiante'
;
QUIT;



DATA PROJECAO_DE_CARGA;
SET POLITICA_BAU

	POLITICA_RECEPTIVO
	POLITICA_DESAFIANTE_RENTABILIZAR;

if VrLimiteContaCartaoGeral > LIMITE_ATUAL then limite_oculto = 1;
RUN;



proc freq data=PROJECAO_DE_CARGA; table descricao*limite_oculto / missing nocol nocum norow nopercent; run;


PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE PROJECAO_DE_CARGA_FIM AS SELECT
	A.*,
	B.CLUSTER_CORES_V2,
	B.GRUPO_RISCO_lake2
FROM PROJECAO_DE_CARGA A
LEFT JOIN MANUTLIM._20250519_BASE_FIM_POL_AUMENTO AS B
ON A.CdDimContaCartao = B.CdDimContaCartao
;
RUN;


PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE EXPECTATIVA_AUMENTOS AS SELECT
DESCRICAO,
CLUSTER_CORES_V2,
GRUPO_RISCO_lake2,
COUNT(CdDimContaCartao) AS QTD_CONTAS,
SUM(LIMITE_ATUAL) AS LIMITE_ATUAL,
SUM(LIMITE_NOVO) AS LIMITE_NOVO
FROM PROJECAO_DE_CARGA_FIM
GROUP BY 1,2,3;
RUN;

/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
DATA MANUTLIM.BASE_AUM_FUNC_20250819 (COMPRESS=BINARY) ;
SET FUNCIONARIOS_16;

IF FL_CASCATA = '04.CREDITO ATIVO'
	AND ( FILTROS_DUROS IN ('10. ELEGIVEIS', '10. ELEGIVEIS / COMPLIANCE'))
THEN FL_ACAO = 1;
ELSE FL_ACAO = 0;

LIMITE_FINAL = LIMITE_PROPOSTA;
DELTA = LIMITE_FINAL - LIMITE_ATUAL_2;

RUN;


PROC FREQ DATA=MANUTLIM.BASE_AUM_FUNC_20250819;
TABLE
	FILTROS_DUROS*FL_CASCATA
	FILTROS_DUROS*FL_ACAO
	FILTROS_DUROS*RESUMO_LIMITE_PROPOSTA
	FILTROS_DUROS*FL_ABERTURA_CRED
	FILTROS_DUROS*FL_APROVADO_COMPLIANCE
	FILTROS_DUROS*CDPRODUTO
/ MISSING NOCOL NOROW NOCUM NOPERCENT;
RUN;


PROC MEANS DATA=MANUTLIM.BASE_AUM_FUNC_20250819
N MIN Q1 MEDIAN Q3 MAX MEAN SUM;
VAR LIMITE_ATUAL_2 DELTA LIMITE_FINAL;
CLASS nu_nivel_cargo;
WHERE FL_ACAO = 1;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CUBO AS SELECT
	FILTROS_DUROS,
	FL_ACAO,
	NU_NIVEL_CARGO,
	COUNT(*) AS QTD,
	SUM(LIMITE_ATUAL_2) AS LIMITE_ATUAL,
	SUM(DELTA) AS DELTA,
	SUM(LIMITE_FINAL) AS LIMITE_FINAL
FROM MANUTLIM.BASE_AUM_FUNC_20250819
GROUP BY 1,2,3;
RUN;

/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
DATA ELEG_TESTE_DARWIN;
SET manutlim._20251117_BASE_FIM_POL_RECEPT;

WHERE motivo_recusa = '19_Descanso'
	AND cdsistemaorigem NOT IN ('BNK')
	AND FL_func ^= 1
	AND ESTAGIO NOT IN ('2','3')
	AND FL_OPF ^= 1
	AND FL_GH_PD_101_104 ^= 1
	AND FL_CLIENTE_ELO ^= 1
	AND OFERTA_OCULTA ^= 1
	AND flag_ja_atendido = 0
	AND flag_limite_novo = ""
	AND QtIdadeContaCartao > 6
	AND flag_Controle_recep ne 'Controle'
	AND Flag_atraso_interno in (0,.);

RUN;

PROC FREQ DATA=ELEG_TESTE_DARWIN; TABLE MOTIVO_RECUSA; RUN;

PROC SURVEYSELECT DATA=ELEG_TESTE_DARWIN METHOD=SRS N=10
SEED=2025 OUT=_TESTE_EXCECAO_10;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE TESTE_EXCECAO_10 AS
SELECT
A.CdDimContaCartao,
a.nucpf AS CPF,
a.VrLimiteContaCartaoGeral,
a.limite_atual as LIMITE_ATUAL,
A.limite_receptivo5 as LIMITE_NOVO,

a.bandeira,
'RECEPTIVO' AS DESCRICAO

FROM _TESTE_EXCECAO_10 AS A
;
QUIT;


DATA GALDINO.TESTE_EXCECAO_10_NOVEMBRO25;
SET TESTE_EXCECAO_10;
RUN;
